Gallery index

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

A_only is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged pipeline._report_text helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside <pre> blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

01. s1301_w00_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: ea480afa85f2d00e80329940abe2a386baea34358db9fb9e7b041053da679550.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 provides fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Current (copied prior) estimates: at n0 blue mass is concentrated on cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red mass on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); n1 and n2 are uniform for both roles; n3 blue peaks at (1,1) and red at (0,2). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events). They differ mainly at n0: all three candidate records place the blue peak at (0,2) and the red peak at (0,0), with k0 and k2 showing very high blue peak probability (~0.99) and k1 showing a slightly lower blue peak (~0.89); red peak probability near 0.97–0.999 across those commands. Nodes n1 and n2 in the candidates match the uniform copied prior distributions; n3 candidates match the current n3 peaks. Origins and times are shown in the data; copied prior records are not new estimates and conditional command records are conditional forecasts.

02. s1301_w00_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: c4cd933e0c9c362ff4f28bc09236f7e05d81888d68fe05cef4fbb30c5e7d3861.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains current (inherited/copy) distributions for represented_time 12 and three conditional command estimates (k0,k1,k2) for represented_time 13; copied prior records are not new estimates and command records are conditional scenarios, not executed events. Current, p0: node n0’s blue mass concentrates on four adjacent cells (row 0–1, cols 2–3) while red mass concentrates on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1. Nodes n1 and n2 are uniform in current and in copied prior records. Node n3’s current distributions place blue weight peaked at (0,3) and red peaked at (0,2). Conditional command estimates differ at n0 and n0’s roles: for k0 and k2 blue and red are highly concentrated at (0,2) and (0,0) respectively (very large peak probabilities), while k1 shows the same modal cells but with slightly lower peak probabilities; node n3’s conditional estimates consistently peak at blue (0,3) and red (0,2). No field measurements, presences, subjective states or outside information are inferred.

11. s1301_w00_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Source: A; view: joined; variant: prospective_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 7dfc4e4ff889b80a3e2f566f019a639b4db8e656bc741d63784b84b78de89dcc.

Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) with current and conditional candidate distributions. Copied prior records and inherited entries are not new estimates; native conditional records labeled by command (k0,k1,k2) are conditional forecasts, not executed events. Current (panel p0 and p1) summaries: node n0 — blue mass concentrated on top-right quadrant cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) (peak probability ~0.199); red mass concentrated on top-left quadrant cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) (peak probability ~0.229). Nodes n1 and n2 in current views are uniform over the 4x4 grid for both roles. Node n3 in current views — red peaks at (0,2) (~0.979); blue peaks at (1,1) (~0.367). Conditional candidate records differ: at n0, commands k0–k2 concentrate blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0) with higher peak probabilities (k0/k2 ~0.996; k1 ~0.946 for blue; red peaks ~0.86–0.99), reflecting command-conditional shifts from the copied current distributions.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Panel-level current records (view=current) are copied prior snapshots and are not new estimates; candidate records (view=candidate, commands k0/k1/k2) are conditional alternatives, not events that have occurred. In both panels the current distributions: n0 — blue peaks at cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); red peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). n1 and n2 are uniform across the grid. n3 — blue peaks at (1,1); red peaks at (0,2). Candidate command records show alternatives mainly for n0: k0 and k2 concentrate blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0); k1 also concentrates similarly but with different peak probabilities. Many candidate entries for n1 and n2 remain uniform. No propositions here assert physical presence, only which grid cells are maxima of the reported distributions.

15. s1301_w00_t12_ordinary_A_only_baseline

Source: ordinary; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: e43eab6c9051d585b94f6f5d7d8907f30cc72b5de5d8d595c83ff4268229ce00.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible location distributions (4x4 grid, rows downward, columns rightward) for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panel p0. Current estimates (represented time 12) copy prior records for nodes n1–n2 and inherit history for n3; copied prior records are not new estimates. Current: at n0 blue probability concentrates on the 2x2 block at columns 2–3 and rows 0–1 (peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)); red concentrates on the 2x2 block at columns 0–1 and rows 0–1 (peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). Nodes n1 and n2 are uniform in the current view (all 16 cells tied). Node n3 current peaks: blue at (1,1), red at (0,2). Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) present alternative conditional estimates for n0–n3; these are conditional command estimates (not events that already occurred). For n0 the candidate commands each place single-cell peaks for blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0), with varying peak probabilities across k0/k1/k2. For n1–n2 candidates are uniform; for n3 candidates match the single-cell peaks in the current record.

24. s1301_w00_t12_ordinary_joined_current_restored

Source: ordinary; view: joined; variant: current_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 5c99421f2a48a7fc4af1b041f43d3a66717f4e223a0a1ed65fe121852a5c3eee.

Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels (p0 and p1). Each panel gives current distributions and conditional command (k0,k1,k2) candidate distributions at nodes n0–n3 for red and blue on a 4×4 grid (rows downward, columns rightward). Copied prior records are presented as inherited current values and are not new estimates; native conditional records are conditional forecasts, not executed events. Current (both panels): n0 — blue mass concentrated on the top-right 2×2 block (cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]); red mass concentrated on the top-left 2×2 block ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]). n1 and n2 — uniform distributions across all 16 cells for both roles. n3 — blue peak at [1,1]; red peak at [0,2]. Conditional command records change the n0 and n0-related estimates across panels and commands: some candidate records (e.g. several k0/k1/k2 entries) collapse blue and red to single-cell peaks at [0,2] (blue) and [0,0] (red) with higher peak probabilities, while other conditional records (and the current view in p1) keep the broader 2×2 pattern at n0. Other nodes remain uniform or retain the same single-cell peaks shown in current. No propositions here assert physical presence or event occurrence; these are descriptions of the supplied distributions and their peak cells.

27. s1301_w00_t12_ordinary_joined_W_changed

Source: ordinary; view: joined; variant: W_changed.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 5bbad75eab44bb67100a3f25c8be70946de40ed42b13614023bfa37a19cd436b.

Narrator text:

This packet reports fallible location distributions on 4×4 local grids for red and blue roles at four addressed nodes, in two panels (p0,p1). Panel-level current views copy prior records (not new estimates); candidate entries are conditional command estimates (not executed events). Current, panel p0: node n0 — blue mass concentrated on four top-right cells (rows 0–1, cols 2–3), red mass concentrated on four top-left cells (rows 0–1, cols 0–1). Nodes n1 and n2 are uniform in current p0. Node n3 in current p0 has a blue peak at (1,1) and a red peak at (0,2). Panel p1 current matches p0 for n0 and shows uniform distributions at n1–n3. Across conditional command records, node n0 distributions vary by command: many candidate entries place blue peak at (0,2) and red peak at (0,0) with differing sharpness; some candidate snapshots show the multi-cell pattern for n0. Copied prior records and conditional records are presented as data only; no physical presence or event occurrence is asserted.

Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at four addressed nodes (n0–n3). Origins and times are as supplied: copied prior records are inherited, and conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are hypothetical estimates, not executed events. Current estimates (view=current) are the present distributions: in both panels n0’s blue peaks are the 2×2 block at (rows 0–1, cols 2–3) and red peaks are the 2×2 block at (rows 0–1, cols 0–1); n1 and n2 are uniform across the grid for both roles; n3’s blue peak is cell [1,1] and red peak is cell [0,2]. Candidate records show alternative conditional peaks at n0 and n3: k0/k1/k2 for p0 and p1 place blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0] (n0) and blue [1,1], red [0,2] (n3), while some candidate branches are uniform. Differences across command-conditioned records are captured in the listed candidate propositions; no physical facts or outside inferences are asserted.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 holds fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Copied prior records (current) are not new estimates; candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional alternatives and are not events that have occurred. Current distributions: at n0 blue peaks are the four cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red peaks are the four cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). Nodes n1–n3 current distributions are uniform for both roles. Candidate differences: k0 is uniform at all nodes; k1 concentrates at n0 with blue peak (0,2) and red peak (0,0) while leaving other nodes uniform; k2 places the same n0 2×2 peaks as current (blue top-right 2×2, red top-left 2×2) and is uniform elsewhere.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 current estimates (copied prior records): at node n0 the red distribution concentrates on the top-left 2x2 block (peaks at [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; peak probability ~0.229) and the blue distribution concentrates on the top-right 2x2 block (peaks at [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; peak probability ~0.199). Nodes n1–n3 current distributions for both roles are uniform over all 16 grid cells. Candidate command records are conditional, not executed events: under command k0 n0’s candidate peaks match the current n0 pattern; under k2 n0’s candidate distributions are much more concentrated to single cells (blue peak at [0,2], red peak at [0,0], with much higher peak probabilities). Other candidate entries (k0, k1, k2 for nodes n1–n3 and k1 for n0) remain uniform. Copied prior records are inherited, not new estimates; command-origin entries are conditional estimates at their represented times.

38. s1301_w00_t12_analytic_joined_current_restored

Source: analytic; view: joined; variant: current_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 56c9b4af4843b32b783768a4f424cb24a98e307ee5a7326826346426a9bd5288.

Narrator text:

This packet contains location-distribution data for red and blue roles on 4x4 local grids in panels p0 and p1. Current views (represented times provided in the packet) show identical patterns in both panels: at node n0 blue mass concentrates on the four top-right cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) while red mass concentrates on the four top-left cells ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); nodes n1–n3 are uniform for both roles. Candidate command records are conditional, not executed events; copied prior records are inherited, not new estimates. Across candidates: k0 reproduces the n0 concentration matching the current pattern; k1 is uniform everywhere; k2 represents a sharp conditional concentration at n0 with blue peaking at [0,2] and red peaking at [0,0] (single-cell peaks with much larger peak probabilities than the current four-cell peaks); nodes n1–n3 remain uniform under all candidates.

40. s1301_w00_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_restored


Source: analytic; view: joined; variant: prospective_restored.


Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.


Narrator text SHA-256: e697c21ba6fad1e33629757e3bd22e1669c4295c980e523c37ac28e9c6e750bd.


Narrator text:


Two panels (p0, p1) provide 4×4 discrete location distributions for red and blue at four nodes (n0–n3). Current distributions (view=current) are inherited/copied records and are not new estimates: for both panels n0 shows concentrated mass on the upper-left 2×2 block for red (peak cells [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]) and on the upper-right 2×2 block for blue (peak cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]); nodes n1–n3 are uniform 4×4 for both roles. Conditional command records (candidate view) are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Across candidate commands: k1 yields uniform distributions at all nodes; k0 matches the current concentration at n0 and uniform elsewhere; k2 places very sharp single-cell peaks at n0 (blue peak [0,2], red peak [0,0]) with other nodes uniform. The packet reports only these distributional estimates and their peak cell sets.

43. s1301_w00_t12_W_W_only_baseline

Narrator text SHA-256: 5ebfa9f6f0fe905291b5f526da6b463fa22d9b5f040879c546c019cc9e68a2b2.


Narrator text:


Panel p0 provides fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (completed_record, represented_time=12) summaries: at n0 blue mass is concentrated on the four cells (row,col) (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) (peak probability ≈0.1986; expected column ≈2.09, expected row ≈0.50); red at n0 concentrates on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) (peak ≈0.2289; expected column ≈0.67, expected row ≈0.50). At nodes n1, n2 and n3 current distributions for both roles are uniform over all 16 cells (peak probability 0.0625; expected row and column 1.5). Candidate records are conditional estimates (native_conditional_record represented_time=13 or inherited_current represented_time=13) and are not executed events. Differences across commands: k0 reproduces the current concentrated n0 pattern and uniform others; k1 gives uniform distributions at all nodes including n0; k2 includes a conditional branch that sharply peaks at n0: blue peak at (0,2) (peak≈0.9986) and red peak at (0,0) (peak≈0.9959), while n1–n3 remain uniform. Copied/inherited current entries are not new independent observations; conditional command entries are conditional estimates, not statements that those commands were executed.

Narrator text:


Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions over 4×4 local grids for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions are archived/inherited snapshots (represented_time 12, archived_origin_time 13) and are not new estimates; candidate records (commands k0, k1, k2) are conditional native records (represented_time 13) and are not events that have actually occurred. Current, panel p0: at n0 red mass concentrates on the top-left 2×2 cells and blue mass concentrates on the top-right 2×2 cells; at n1–n3 both red and blue distributions are uniform across all 16 cells. Candidate k0 matches the n0 concentrations for p0 and leaves n1–n3 uniform. Candidate k1 makes n0 uniform (as do n1–n3). Candidate k2 makes n0 sharply peaked for blue at cell [0,2] and for red at [0,0], while n1–n3 remain uniform. Differences across candidate views are limited to n0; other nodes retain uniform distributions in all candidates.
